The market structure in the developed countries, which are ready for foreign capital, is more effective than in the developing countries, and provides more protection for the coming capital, so they attract more investment. Development countries can bring more restrictions on foreign capital investments -- such as chain markets -- according to developing countries -- but allow capital investment, not according to the capital's demand, but according to their own socioeconomic structures. For example, there are restrictions in France, Germany, Italy, and nowhere are chain markets as free as they are in our country. Again, like so many countries around the world, foreign capital has been brought to some ease in our country. For example, in 1986, institutional tax was reduced by 46 per cent to 20 per cent today. It is clear that chain markets and supermarkets, whose financial income is open to many doubts in our country, are not also contributing to social structure. As the National Movement Party, we have supported any attempt to prevent environmental pollution by this time, and we will continue to support it. It is specifically intended to prevent inorganic waste from polluting the environment in this way, with the deposit management system. In these packaging materials, recycling is particularly important. Glass is the healthiest wrapping material. You need to increase the use of glass. Wrapping materials that should not be used are plastic materials. Considering that plastic materials have been dissolved in the soil for at least four hundred years, you certainly shouldn't allow plastics to contaminate the soil as waste. In fact, it is also important that the microbes that appear during the dissolution of plastic waste enter fish's organisms and the fish become toxic. Therefore, you must be sensitive about waste. The ideal is zero waste; zero waste is a culture, a lifestyle. Collecting it at home and recycling it is important for both the environment and the economy to prevent pollution. Therefore, in society, to create waste culture, collect waste, and achieve the ideal of zero waste, we see the need for awareness, education. With that consciousness, we need to develop waste recycling in our society. But we're not supposed to do anything: we're taking plastic waste especially from the outside, importing it, we have to give it up. You must not allow plastic waste removal activities from China. I think we should stop this waste import, especially considering that plastic waste is very dirty at this time. Another issue that exists in the proposal of the law is the arrangements for building bicycles and scooters to reduce exhaust emissions. And we support that, as the National Movement Party, we find it very useful. Because one of the main reasons for air pollution is exhaust emissions, 20 percent of air pollution depends on exhaust emissions. No matter how much we reduce this, no matter how much we prevent air pollution, we prevent global warming and climate change. There is no recycling of the damage related to global warming and climate change. The less we reduce this, the more useful we can be for our world, for our future. Another issue in the proposal of the law is the regulation for stopping activities without an environmental impact assessment report, without a CED report. It's also very important, and it's even important to make CED practice, to make CED report practice very common, even in every activity, in every policy. It's a job to define the impact of everything on the environment, and it's the CED arrangement, and it's the indication of the damage to the environment, and it's the process of taking measures to prevent that damage. For this reason, I think we need to expand the decision on the provision of the CED report, ask for a CED report in all activities, and be thorough in carrying out this report. As the National Movement Party, we support all arrangements for the environment, and I say we will continue to support it from now on. I wish you the best of the law offer. Thank you.
